# Mersenne factors, orders and Frobenius

GAP 4.17dev. Prime q range: 97 ≤ q ≤ 100000000 (inclusive).
The range is a computational choice, not an upper bound from the notes.
Structural tables for the remaining case of `prime_power_notes.tex`: A > 1, q > 3, q does not divide M.
Retain q mod 240 in {1,97,193}, c=v₂(q−1) even, and r=ord_q(2) dividing (q−1)/6. Bound E=lcm(2,r) for complete factorization. Subsequent failures are displayed, not filtered out.
Set rho=v_q(2^r−1), a=(2^E−1)/q^rho, u=(q−1)/2^c. In a solution, rho=n₁ must be even. An odd rho is recorded as a failure, not renamed n₁.

## Counts after successive conditions

| Condition retained cumulatively | Number of q |
| --- | --- |
| q prime; q mod 240 in {1,97,193} | 269952 |
| c=v₂(q−1) even (hence c≥4) | 179902 |
| 2^((q−1)/6) = 1 modulo q; equivalently r divides (q−1)/6 | 59931 |
| E ≤ 256; rows tabulated below | 18 |

Among all 59931 q passing the first three conditions (without the E bound), 0 satisfy 2^(q−1)=1 modulo q².
Thus none of the present rows is a candidate for the full system.

## q = 433

| q | c = v₂(q−1) | rho = v_q(2^r−1) | r | E | ord_(q²)(2) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 433 | 4 | 1 | 72 | 72 | 31176 |

Failed additional conditions: rho odd; q != 1 mod 7 (3 divides E); q != 1 mod 5 (4 divides E); q != 1 mod 17 (8 divides E); q != 1 mod 13 (12 divides E); q != 1 mod 19 (18 divides E).

q−1 = 2^4 * 3^3; ordinary odd prime divisors of q−1: [ 3 ].

## Table definitions and coverage

The q rows exhaust the retained conditions with q ≤ 100000000 and E ≤ 256. The N tables cover every 5 ≤ N ≤ 121 with gcd(N,6E)=1. Neither bound is asserted for the original problem.
The N rows display the order test L divides N and the product test D_E divides uN, even when they fail. No M, h or p is chosen. Conditions requiring those choices are not tested.
Here L=ord_a(q), computed as the lcm of the prime-power orders. A nonunit gcd(L,6E) excludes every N with gcd(N,6E)=1 for that row.
S_ℓ consists of primes s dividing j=(ℓ−1)/ord_ℓ(2) with s not dividing 6E. For every N with gcd(N,6E)=1, the full factor ℓ^f is omitted from D_E exactly when some s in S_ℓ divides N. Empty S_ℓ means that ℓ^f is always retained. This description has no upper bound on N.
The Frobenius tables include all s occurring in the N evaluations and every s in every S_ℓ, including those above the N range.
An ordinary odd prime divisor ℓ of q−1 means v_ℓ(2^ord_ℓ(2)−1)=1. Such a prime cannot divide N in a solution. The column records this test separately.
The indices d in each factor table distinguish the primitive exponent t=ord_ℓ(2) from other cyclotomic values Φ_d(2) containing ℓ. The q factor itself is removed completely; its primitive exponent is r.
Factorization products and the primality of every factor are checked. Above 2^64, a GAP primality proof is required.

## Frobenius and the selected factors

For ℓ not dividing m, arithmetic Frobenius in Q(ζ_m) sends ζ_m to ζ_m^ℓ; its order is ord_m(ℓ), the residue degree. See [Milne, Algebraic Number Theory, cyclotomic extensions and Frobenius](https://www.jmilne.org/math/CourseNotes/ANT.pdf).
For t=ord_ℓ(2), ℓ=1 modulo t, so Frobenius in Q(ζ_t) is the identity. To form D_E, use Q(ζ_s) for each prime s dividing N. Since gcd(E,N)=1 and t divides E, gcd((ℓ−1)/t,N)=gcd(ℓ−1,N).
Thus ℓ^f belongs to D_E precisely when Frobenius is nonidentity in every Q(ζ_s) with s dividing N and s≠ℓ. At s=ℓ the prime is ramified; it imposes no exclusion from D_E, since ℓ does not divide ℓ−1. The table retains the full ℓ^f, and checks D_E divides ((q−1)/2^c)N.
For ℓ≠s, Φ_s over F_ℓ has (s−1)/f distinct irreducible factors of degree f=ord_s(ℓ); these correspond to the prime ideals above ℓ. At ℓ=s, Φ_s=(X−1)^(s−1) over F_s. The displayed decomposition data can therefore be checked by finite-field factorization.
The order in Q(ζ_N) alone is insufficient to select D_E: the restrictions to each Q(ζ_s) matter. This is the exact role of the additional cyclotomic moduli here.

## Reproduction

```sh
~/fluid/sauce/gap/gap -A -q -b mersenne_tables.g
```

To change the bounds, in GAP:

```gap
PPConfig := rec(qMax := 10^8, nMax := 121, factorE := 256,
                output := "mersenne-tables.md");;
Read("mersenne_tables.g");
```

`mersenne_tables_test.g` checks the arithmetic and table helpers on independent small fixtures; those fixtures are not included as candidates.
